- **station**: The base behavior of every session of a sofa team (a station). Each role session loads it at start, before its role files. It holds the rules that are the same for all roles: stay in your role, ask the brains first, the message format, the turn exit rule, blockers, worktrees, the state store, and the Proofs block of each PR. Use when a start prompt says "You are the &lt;role&gt; of the &lt;prefix&gt; team", or when a sofa role file names it. ([ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins](https://github.com/ai-advanced-futures/cc-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/sofa/skills/station))
- **station**: Use this skill when building with the Station background job framework. Station is a TypeScript-first framework for type-safe background jobs with Zod validation. ([porkytheblack/glorp](https://github.com/porkytheblack/glorp/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/station))

- **statistical-computing**: Computational tools and algorithms for statistical analysis. Covers simulation, resampling methods (bootstrap, permutation tests), Monte Carlo methods, random number generation, numerical optimization (Newton-Raphson, EM algorithm), cross-validation, and reproducible analysis workflows.
